        {
            "id": "05e8e6bb-88a3-43cc-bf94-8ebad92e8cc0",
            "author": {
                "role": "assistant",
                "name": null,
                "metadata": {}
            },
            "create_time": 1791887460.911902,
            "update_time": 1791887482.495387,
            "content": {
                "content_type": "text",
                "parts": [
                    "Let's find a property in **Noida Sector 1 (Noida Extension / Greater Noida West)** that fits your requirements, rather than looking at random projects across Noida.\n\nFrom our earlier property discussions, I'll use these as the starting point:\n\n- **Purpose:** Buying for self-use.\n- **Budget:** Up to ₹2 crore.\n- **Preferred configuration:** 3BHK.\n- **Lifestyle priorities:** Plenty of open space, good schools nearby, and a shopping mall within convenient reach.\n\nI'll prioritize properties actually located in Sector 1, and keep nearby sectors separate so you can compare them without confusing the locations.\n\n## Properties worth shortlisting first\n\nThese are current online listing leads, not verified offers. Advertised prices and availability can change, and some listings may quote base prices before additional charges.\n\n<box gap={4}>\n  <row align=start gap={3}>\n    <AsyncImage query=\"The Valley residential apartment project Sector 1 Greater Noida West\" aspectRatio=\"3:4\" maxWidth=\"124px\"/>\n    <box flex=\"1\" gap={1}>\n      <title size=\"lg\">1. <Entity value=\"The Valley\" category=\"point_of_interest\" location=\"Sector 1, Greater Noida West, Uttar Pradesh, India\"/></title>\n      <row align=center gap={2} wrap=\"wrap\">\n        <badge color=\"success\">First to investigate</badge>\n        <text color=\"secondary\" size=\"sm\">3BHK</text>\n      </row>\n      <title size=\"xl\" color=\"success\">₹1.8 crore</title>\n      <text color=\"secondary\" size=\"sm\">Advertised area: 1,830 sq ft</text>\n      Worth checking if you want a larger apartment within your budget. Confirm the exact unit, usable carpet area, open-space views and possession status. <Cite refs={[\"turn677719search0\",\"turn677719search1\"]}/>\n      <text><Link url=\"https://housing.com/in/buy/greater-noida/sector-1-greater-noida-west-noida-extension-gid/3bhk-flats-fid/\" title=\"View current listings\"/></text>\n    </box>\n  </row>\n  <divider color=\"subtle\"/>\n  <row align=start gap={3}>\n    <AsyncImage query=\"Nirala Grand residential society Sector 1 Greater Noida West apartment towers\" aspectRatio=\"3:4\" maxWidth=\"124px\"/>\n    <box flex=\"1\" gap={1}>\n      <title size=\"lg\">2. <Entity value=\"Nirala Grand\" category=\"point_of_interest\" location=\"Sector 1, Greater Noida West, Uttar Pradesh, India\"/></title>\n      <title size=\"xl\">₹1.6–2.3 crore</title>\n      <text color=\"secondary\" size=\"sm\">Advertised 3BHK listings</text>\n      A candidate for comparing larger or differently configured units. Some advertised options exceed your ₹2 crore ceiling, so focus on units whose total cost stays within budget. <Cite refs={[\"turn677719search0\",\"turn677719search4\"]}/>\n      <text><Link url=\"https://housing.com/in/buy/greater-noida/sector-1-greater-noida-west-noida-extension-gid/3bhk-flats-fid/\" title=\"View available units\"/></text>\n    </box>\n  </row>\n  <divider color=\"subtle\"/>\n  <row align=start gap={3}>\n    <AsyncImage query=\"RG Pleiaddes residential apartment project Greater Noida West\" aspectRatio=\"3:4\" maxWidth=\"124px\"/>\n    <box flex=\"1\" gap={1}>\n      <title size=\"lg\">3. <Entity value=\"RG Pleiaddes\" category=\"point_of_interest\" location=\"Greater Noida West, Uttar Pradesh, India\"/></title>\n      <title size=\"xl\">₹1.4 crore</title>\n      <text color=\"secondary\" size=\"sm\">Advertised 3BHK listing</text>\n      A potentially more budget-conscious option. Listings place it in Noida Extension, but confirm its precise sector and address before considering it a Sector 1 match. <Cite refs={[\"turn677719search0\",\"turn677719search1\"]}/>\n      <text><Link url=\"https://housing.com/in/buy/greater-noida/sector-1-greater-noida-west-noida-extension-gid/3bhk-flats-fid/\" title=\"Check listings\"/></text>\n    </box>\n  </row>\n  <divider color=\"subtle\"/>\n  <row align=start gap={3}>\n    <AsyncImage query=\"Stellar Jeevan residential apartment society Sector 1 Greater Noida West\" aspectRatio=\"3:4\" maxWidth=\"124px\"/>\n    <box flex=\"1\" gap={1}>\n      <title size=\"lg\">4. <Entity value=\"Stellar Jeevan\" ref=\"turn677719business2\"/></title>\n      <text color=\"secondary\" size=\"sm\">Existing residential society</text>\n      A society to investigate for resale opportunities. Its listed location is Sector 1; I haven't verified a currently available 3BHK or its asking price.\n      <text><Link url=\"https://housing.com/in/buy/greater-noida/sector-1-greater-noida-west-noida-extension-gid/3bhk-flats-fid/\" title=\"Search resale listings\"/></text>\n    </box>\n  </row>\n</box>\n\n<MapWidgetV2 points={[{\"ref\":\"turn677719business2\"},{\"ref\":\"turn677719business12\"},{\"ref\":\"turn677719business0\"},{\"ref\":\"turn677719business13\"},{\"ref\":\"turn677719business6\"}]} title=\"Sector 1 residential societies\" carousel={{\"visibility\":\"hidden\"}}/>\n\n## Before I narrow this to the best 2–3 options\n\nI need to know one thing that will substantially change the shortlist: **when do you want to move in?**\n\n{@body const [moveIn,setMoveIn] = DIL.useState(\"ready\")}\n{@body const [budget,setBudget] = DIL.useState(\"2cr\")}\n{@body const [priority,setPriority] = DIL.useState(\"open\")}\n<box border radius=\"xl\" padding={4} gap={4}>\n  <text weight=\"medium\">1. Possession timeline</text>\n  <radio-group value={moveIn} onChange={setMoveIn} direction=\"col\">\n    <radio value=\"ready\">Ready to move or resale only</radio>\n    <radio value=\"1year\">Within the next 12 months</radio>\n    <radio value=\"underconstruction\">Under-construction is fine</radio>\n  </radio-group>\n  <divider color=\"subtle\"/>\n  <text weight=\"medium\">2. Is ₹2 crore your all-inclusive budget?</text>\n  <radio-group value={budget} onChange={setBudget} direction=\"col\">\n    <radio value=\"2cr\">Yes, including registration and other charges</radio>\n    <radio value=\"base2cr\">₹2 crore for the flat, charges extra</radio>\n    <radio value=\"flexible\">I can stretch a little for the right property</radio>\n  </radio-group>\n  <divider color=\"subtle\"/>\n  <text weight=\"medium\">3. What matters most?</text>\n  <radio-group value={priority} onChange={setPriority} direction=\"col\">\n    <radio value=\"open\">Open green spaces and less crowded surroundings</radio>\n    <radio value=\"schools\">Schools and family convenience</radio>\n    <radio value=\"mall\">Mall, shops and daily conveniences</radio>\n    <radio value=\"size\">Maximum apartment size and better layout</radio>\n  </radio-group>\n  <button block onClick={() => GenUI.issueNewTurn(\"Shortlist properties in Noida Sector 1 (Greater Noida West) for self-use, preferably 3BHK, with a maximum budget of ₹2 crore. Possession preference: \"+({\"ready\":\"ready-to-move or resale only\",\"1year\":\"move in within 12 months\",\"underconstruction\":\"under-construction acceptable\"}[moveIn])+\". Budget interpretation: \"+({\"2cr\":\"₹2 crore all-inclusive\",\"base2cr\":\"₹2 crore base price plus charges\",\"flexible\":\"slightly flexible for the right property\"}[budget])+\". Main priority: \"+({\"open\":\"open green spaces and less crowded surroundings\",\"schools\":\"nearby schools and family convenience\",\"mall\":\"nearby malls and daily conveniences\",\"size\":\"maximum apartment size and layout\"}[priority])+\". Give verified current listings, precise sector, asking price, carpet area, possession, RERA and nearby schools/malls. Separate unverified claims from verified facts.\")}>Find my best options</button>\n</box>\n\nOnce you answer, I'll narrow the search around your actual requirements instead of relying on project advertisements alone."
                ]
            },
            "status": "finished_successfully",
            "end_turn": true,

                    "fallbackMarkdown": "Let's find a property in **Noida Sector 1 (Noida Extension / Greater Noida West)** that fits your requirements, rather than looking at random projects across Noida.\n\nFrom our earlier property discussions, I'll use these as the starting point\\:\n\n- **Purpose\\:** Buying for self-use.\n- **Budget\\:** Up to ₹2 crore.\n- **Preferred configuration\\:** 3BHK.\n- **Lifestyle priorities\\:** Plenty of open space, good schools nearby, and a shopping mall within convenient reach.\n\nI'll prioritize properties actually located in Sector 1, and keep nearby sectors separate so you can compare them without confusing the locations.\n\n## Properties worth shortlisting first\n\nThese are current online listing leads, not verified offers.             "create_time": 1791887525.183514,
            "update_time": 1791887530.43438,
            "content": {
                "content_type": "text",
                "parts": [
                    "Yes, **Noida Sector 1 and Greater Noida West Sector 1 (Noida Extension) are two different locations.** You're asking about Sector 1 in Noida proper, near Sector 15 and the Delhi border.\n\nI checked property listings specifically for this distinction.\n\n**I couldn't verify any major residential apartment project in Noida Sector 1 itself.** Most of the projects appearing in searches for \"Sector 1 Noida\" are actually located in Greater Noida West. <Cite refs={[\"turn610322search5\",\"turn610322search1\"]}/>\n\nHere's the difference:\n\n| | Noida Sector 1 | Greater Noida West Sector 1 |\n|---|---|---|\n| Location | Near Sector 15, Delhi border | Noida Extension |\n| Development | Primarily commercial/industrial | Predominantly residential |\n| Large gated societies | No major project verified | Many available |\n| 3BHK availability | Very limited or unverified | Numerous projects |\n| Suitable for your requirements | Unlikely | More options |\n\nFor your ₹2 crore budget, 3BHK requirement, open spaces and nearby schools, I'd recommend looking at **Noida Sectors 15A, 44, 50, 75, 76 or 78** instead, depending on how far you're willing to move from Sector 1.\n\nOne question: **Do you specifically need Sector 1 because of its location, or are you open to other sectors within Noida proper (excluding Greater Noida and Noida Extension)?**"
                ]
            },
            "status": "finished_successfully",
            "end_turn": true,

                    "fallbackMarkdown": "Yes, **Noida Sector 1 and Greater Noida West Sector 1 (Noida Extension) are two different locations.** You're asking about Sector 1 in Noida proper, near Sector 15 and the Delhi border.\n\nI checked property listings specifically for this distinction.\n\n**I couldn't verify any major residential apartment project in Noida Sector 1 itself.** Most of the projects appearing in searches for \"Sector 1 Noida\" are actually located in Greater Noida West. citeturn610322search5turn610322search1\n\nHere's the difference\\:\n\n|  | Noida Sector 1 | Greater Noida West Sector 1 |\n|--------|--------|--------|\n| Location | Near Sector 15, Delhi border | Noida Extension |\n| Development | Primarily commercial/industrial | Predominantly residential |\n| Large gated societies | No major project verified | Many available |\n| 3BHK availability | Very limited or unverified | Numerous projects |\n| Suitable for your requirements | Unlikely | More options |\n\nFor your ₹2 crore budget, 3BHK requirement, open spaces and nearby schools, I'd recommend looking at **Noida Sectors 15A, 44, 50, 75, 76 or 78** instead, depending on how far you're willing to move from Sector 1.\n\nOne question: **Do you specifically need Sector 1 because of its location, or are you open to other sectors within Noida proper (excluding Greater Noida and Noida Extension)?**",

        {
            "id": "b8eb8bcc-4e48-45a5-8cb1-b58102ea2277",
            "author": {
                "role": "assistant",
                "name": null,
                "metadata": {}
            },
            "create_time": 1791887593.597146,
            "update_time": 1791887609.319372,
            "content": {
                "content_type": "text",
                "parts": [
                    "If your goal is to invest in Noida property today and sell in around 10 years, I'd focus on **where prices can still grow, where genuine demand will come from, and how easily you can resell the property** rather than simply choosing the sector with the biggest advertised appreciation.\n\nGiven your earlier ₹2 crore budget and preference for a 3BHK, there's another factor worth preserving: you may eventually want to live in the property, and your office is in Sector 63. That makes established central Noida sectors more relevant than locations that depend heavily on future infrastructure development.\n\nNo sector can be guaranteed to deliver the highest returns by 2036. The sensible approach is to compare likely growth drivers, current entry prices, rental demand and downside risks.\n\n## My shortlist for a 10-year investment\n\n<box gap={3}>\n  <row align=start gap={3}>\n    <AsyncImage query=\"Sector 78 Noida established gated residential apartments greenery\" aspectRatio=\"4:5\" maxWidth=\"112px\"/>\n    <box flex=\"1\" gap={1}>\n      <title size=\"lg\">1. Sector 78 — best overall balance</title>\n      <badge>My first choice for you</badge>\n      Established residential societies, metro access, schools and everyday amenities make this a balanced choice for both investment and eventual self-use. Recent portal estimates put average asking prices around ₹12,000–₹13,000/sq ft, although project-level variation is substantial. <Cite refs={[\"turn515096search4\",\"turn515096search2\"]}/>\n      <text color=\"secondary\" size=\"sm\">Main risk: you may pay a premium for an already-developed location, limiting future upside.</text>\n    </box>\n  </row>\n  <divider color=\"subtle\"/>\n  <row align=start gap={3}>\n    <AsyncImage query=\"Sector 137 Noida residential apartment society metro station green landscaped complex\" aspectRatio=\"4:5\" maxWidth=\"112px\"/>\n    <box flex=\"1\" gap={1}>\n      <title size=\"lg\">2. Sector 137 — strongest alternative for rental demand</title>\n      Close to the Noida–Greater Noida Expressway, Aqua Line metro and employment hubs. Existing housing and a working commuter market make it worth considering for rental income alongside appreciation. <Cite refs={[\"turn515096search5\",\"turn515096search7\"]}/>\n      <text color=\"secondary\" size=\"sm\">Main risk: expressway traffic, project-specific maintenance and competition between rental properties.</text>\n    </box>\n  </row>\n  <divider color=\"subtle\"/>\n  <row align=start gap={3}>\n    <AsyncImage query=\"Sector 150 Noida residential towers landscaped park sports facilities\" aspectRatio=\"4:5\" maxWidth=\"112px\"/>\n    <box flex=\"1\" gap={1}>\n      <title size=\"lg\">3. Sector 150 — higher-upside, higher-risk candidate</title>\n      Planned green spaces, sports-oriented development and expressway connectivity make it attractive for a long holding period. However, the Sports City project has faced prolonged regulatory and construction issues. A revised plan was approved in October 2026, but that does not clear every project to proceed. <Cite refs={[\"turn515096news11\",\"turn515096search7\"]}/>\n      <text color=\"secondary\" size=\"sm\">Main risk: approvals, project delivery, premium pricing and slower resale in some developments.</text>\n    </box>\n  </row>\n  <divider color=\"subtle\"/>\n  <row align=start gap={3}>\n    <AsyncImage query=\"Sector 75 Noida modern residential high rise apartments landscaped society\" aspectRatio=\"4:5\" maxWidth=\"112px\"/>\n    <box flex=\"1\" gap={1}>\n      <title size=\"lg\">4. Sector 75 — established residential demand</title>\n      An alternative to Sector 78, with established societies and daily conveniences. Worth comparing for a well-priced resale flat rather than assuming a newer or more expensive project will appreciate faster. <Cite refs={[\"turn515096search0\",\"turn515096search1\"]}/>\n      <text color=\"secondary\" size=\"sm\">Main risk: society condition, maintenance charges and paying too much for a mature market.</text>\n    </box>\n  </row>\n</box>\n\n## What could ₹2 crore become by 2036?\n\nRather than pretending to know which sector will deliver the highest appreciation, use these scenarios to understand the stakes.\n\n<box border radius=\"xl\" padding={4} gap={3}>\n  <text color=\"secondary\" size=\"sm\">Illustrative value of a ₹2 crore property after 10 years</text>\n  <table>\n    <table-row>\n      <table-cell>\n        Annual appreciation assumption\n      </table-cell>\n      <table-cell align=\"end\">\n        Value in 2036\n      </table-cell>\n    </table-row>\n    <table-row>\n      <table-cell>4% per year</table-cell>\n      <table-cell align=\"end\">₹2.96 crore</table-cell>\n    </table-row>\n    <table-row>\n      <table-cell>6% per year</table-cell>\n      <table-cell align=\"end\">₹3.58 crore</table-cell>\n    </table-row>\n    <table-row>\n      <table-cell>8% per year</table-cell>\n      <table-cell align=\"end\">₹4.32 crore</table-cell>\n    </table-row>\n    <table-row>\n      <table-cell>10% per year</table-cell>\n      <table-cell align=\"end\">₹5.19 crore</table-cell>\n    </table-row>\n  </table>\n  <text color=\"secondary\" size=\"xs\">These are mathematical scenarios, not forecasts for Noida. They exclude stamp duty, registration, maintenance, taxes, selling costs, rental income and inflation.</text>\n</box>\n\nThe important point is that a property bought at an inflated price in a supposedly high-growth sector can underperform a fairly priced property in an established sector.\n\n## Compare the locations\n\n<MapWidgetV2 title=\"Your Noida investment shortlist\" points={[{\"ref\":\"turn515096business0\",\"icon\":\"🏡\"},{\"ref\":\"turn515096business2\",\"icon\":\"🏢\"},{\"ref\":\"turn515096business1\",\"icon\":\"🌳\"},{\"ref\":\"turn515096business3\",\"icon\":\"🏙️\"}]} carousel={{\"visibility\":\"hidden\"}}/>\n\nMy final recommendation is **Sector 78 for the best balance of investment and future self-use**, with Sector 137 as the alternative if rental demand matters more. I would consider Sector 150 only after checking the exact project's approvals and total purchase price.\n\nBefore putting ₹2 crore into any property, I would compare actual resale transactions, not just asking prices, and verify the project's RERA status, title, dues, occupancy certificate and builder track record.\n\n**One question that could change my recommendation:** are you planning to invest the full ₹2 crore in one property, or would you consider buying a less expensive property and keeping some money aside for other investments?"
                ]
            },
            "status": "finished_successfully",
            "end_turn": true,

                        "27": "An alternative to Sector 78, with established societies and daily conveniences. Worth comparing for a well-priced resale flat rather than assuming a newer or more expensive project will appreciate faster. ",

                        "31": "What could ₹2 crore become by 2036?",
                        "32": "Rather than pretending to know which sector will deliver the highest appreciation, use these scenarios to understand the stakes.",

                        "46": "Compare the locations",
                        "47": "7f17bcae-f6e1-5c72-b3fe-e70399bc392d",
                        "48": null,
                        "49": "My final recommendation is ",
                        "50": "Sector 78 for the best balance of investment and future self-use",
                        "51": ", with Sector 137 as the alternative if rental demand matters more. I would consider Sector 150 only after checking the exact project's approvals and total purchase price.",
                        "52": "Before putting ₹2 crore into any property, I would compare actual resale transactions, not just asking prices, and verify the project's RERA status, title, dues, occupancy certificate and builder track record.",
                        "53": "One question that could change my recommendation:",
                        "54": " are you planning to invest the full ₹2 crore in one property, or would you consider buying a less expensive property and keeping some money aside for other investments?"
                    },
                    "fallbackMarkdown": "If your goal is to invest in Noida property today and sell in around 10 years, I'd focus on **where prices can still grow, where genuine demand will come from, and how easily you can resell the property** rather than simply choosing the sector with the biggest advertised appreciation.\n\nGiven your earlier ₹2 crore budget and preference for a 3BHK, there's another factor worth preserving: you may eventually want to live in the property, and your office is in Sector 63. That makes established central Noida sectors more relevant than locations that depend heavily on future infrastructure development.\n\nNo sector can be guaranteed to deliver the highest returns by 2036. The sensible approach is to compare likely growth drivers, current entry prices, rental demand and downside risks.\n\n## My shortlist for a 10-year investment\n\nimage_group{\"query\":[\"Sector 78 Noida established gated residential apartments greenery\"],\"aspect_ratio\":\"4:5\",\"layout\":\"carousel\",\"num_per_query\":1}\n\n## 1\\. Sector 78 — best overall balance\n\n(My first choice for you)\n\nEstablished residential societies, metro access, schools and everyday amenities make this a balanced choice for both investment and eventual self-use. Recent portal estimates put average asking prices around ₹12,000–₹13,000/sq ft, although project-level variation is substantial. citeturn515096search4turn515096search2\n\nMain risk: you may pay a premium for an already-developed location, limiting future upside.\n\n---\n\nimage_group{\"query\":[\"Sector 137 Noida residential apartment society metro station green landscaped complex\"],\"aspect_ratio\":\"4:5\",\"layout\":\"carousel\",\"num_per_query\":1}\n\n## 2\\. Sector 137 — strongest alternative for rental demand\n\nClose to the Noida–Greater Noida Expressway, Aqua Line metro and employment hubs. Existing housing and a working commuter market make it worth considering for rental income alongside appreciation. citeturn515096search5turn515096search7\n\nMain risk: expressway traffic, project-specific maintenance and competition between rental properties.\n\n---\n\nimage_group{\"query\":[\"Sector 150 Noida residential towers landscaped park sports facilities\"],\"aspect_ratio\":\"4:5\",\"layout\":\"carousel\",\"num_per_query\":1}\n\n## 3\\. Sector 150 — higher-upside, higher-risk candidate\n\nPlanned green spaces, sports-oriented development and expressway connectivity make it attractive for a long holding period. However, the Sports City project has faced prolonged regulatory and construction issues. A revised plan was approved in October 2026, but that does not clear every project to proceed. citeturn515096news11turn515096search7\n\nMain risk: approvals, project delivery, premium pricing and slower resale in some developments.\n\n---\n\nimage_group{\"query\":[\"Sector 75 Noida modern residential high rise apartments landscaped society\"],\"aspect_ratio\":\"4:5\",\"layout\":\"carousel\",\"num_per_query\":1}\n\n## 4\\. Sector 75 — established residential demand\n\nAn alternative to Sector 78, with established societies and daily conveniences. Worth comparing for a well-priced resale flat rather than assuming a newer or more expensive project will appreciate faster. citeturn515096search0turn515096search1\n\nMain risk: society condition, maintenance charges and paying too much for a mature market.\n\n## What could ₹2 crore become by 2036?\n\nRather than pretending to know which sector will deliver the highest appreciation, use these scenarios to understand the stakes.\n\nIllustrative value of a ₹2 crore property after 10 years\n\n| Annual appreciation assumption | Value in 2036 |\n|--------|--------|\n| 4% per year | ₹2.96 crore |\n| 6% per year | ₹3.58 crore |\n| 8% per year | ₹4.32 crore |\n| 10% per year | ₹5.19 crore |\n\nThese are mathematical scenarios, not forecasts for Noida. They exclude stamp duty, registration, maintenance, taxes, selling costs, rental income and inflation.\n\nThe important point is that a property bought at an inflated price in a supposedly high-growth sector can underperform a fairly priced property in an established sector.\n\n## Compare the locations\ngenui{\"map_widget_v2\":{\"points\":[{\"ref\":\"turn515096business0\",\"icon\":\"🏡\"},{\"ref\":\"turn515096business2\",\"icon\":\"🏢\"},{\"ref\":\"turn515096business1\",\"icon\":\"🌳\"},{\"ref\":\"turn515096business3\",\"icon\":\"🏙️\"}],\"carousel\":{\"visibility\":\"hidden\"},\"title\":\"Your Noida investment shortlist\"}}\n\nMy final recommendation is **Sector 78 for the best balance of investment and future self-use**, with Sector 137 as the alternative if rental demand matters more. I would consider Sector 150 only after checking the exact project's approvals and total purchase price.\n\nBefore putting ₹2 crore into any property, I would compare actual resale transactions, not just asking prices, and verify the project's RERA status, title, dues, occupancy certificate and builder track record.\n\n**One question that could change my recommendation\\:** are you planning to invest the full ₹2 crore in one property, or would you consider buying a less expensive property and keeping some money aside for other investments?",

                                "title": "Noida's new Sports City plan approved: Authority tells SC 70% of land will be reserved for sports facilities as stalled Sector 150 project moves towards revival",
                                "snippet": "The Noida Authority has approved a revised layout plan for the long-delayed 300-acre Sports City project in Sector 150, as reported to the Supreme Court. The new plan designates 70% of the land for sports-related infrastructure, which is a significant step toward reviving the stalled development. This move is expected to provide much-needed relief to homebuyers affected by the delays, although it does not immediately permit full-scale resumption of construction. Additional approvals and decisions on a project-by-project basis are still required. The revised plan was finalized following public objections, and four housing projects within the Sports City have received conditional occupancy certificates. Revised plans are also being submitted for other Sports City developments in Noida. The Supreme Court's involvement underscores the legal and regulatory scrutiny surrounding the project, aiming to ensure transparency and protection for homebuyers.",

        {
            "id": "39bb51b7-7eb7-4ca4-ace1-d57df3112ac5",
            "author": {
                "role": "assistant",
                "name": null,
                "metadata": {}
            },
            "create_time": 1791887675.785996,
            "update_time": 1791887684.110005,
            "content": {
                "content_type": "text",
                "parts": [
                    "There are **two different projects you need to distinguish before looking at the payment plan**:\n\n<box gap={3}>\n  <row align=start gap={3}>\n    <AsyncImage query=\"ACE Parkway completed residential apartment towers Sector 150 Noida\" aspectRatio=\"4:3\" maxWidth=\"116px\"/>\n    <box flex=\"1\" gap={1}>\n      **<Entity ref=\"turn371217business0\" value=\"ACE Parkway\"/> — original project**\n\n      <badge>Ready to move</badge>\n\n      The original project is delivered. If you buy a flat from the developer's remaining inventory, if any, or from an existing owner, the applicable payment terms will differ. The developer's published page lists tower-specific historical plans. <Cite refs={[\"turn530543search5\",\"turn371217search1\"]}/>\n    </box>\n  </row>\n  <divider color=\"subtle\"/>\n  <row align=start gap={3}>\n    <AsyncImage query=\"ACE Arte ACE Parkway 2.0 Sector 150 Noida new luxury residential project architectural rendering\" aspectRatio=\"4:3\" maxWidth=\"116px\"/>\n    <box flex=\"1\" gap={1}>\n      **<Entity ref=\"turn371217business1\" value=\"ACE Arte\"/> — marketed as ACE Parkway 2.0**\n\n      <badge color=\"info\">New launch</badge>\n\n      This is a separate, under-construction project with its own RERA registration and payment schedule. If someone has offered you a new flat with staged payments, this may be the project they mean. <Cite refs={[\"turn371217search1\",\"turn371217search8\"]}/>\n    </box>\n  </row>\n</box>\n\n## 1. ACE Parkway 2.0 / ACE Arte: current published payment plan\n\nThe published plan is called **20×5**, meaning five instalments of 20% of the base price. The first instalment is split into two payments. <Cite refs={[\"turn371217search0\",\"turn371217search8\"]}/>\n\n| Payment stage | Amount due |\n|---|---:|\n| On booking | 10% |\n| Within 30 days of booking | 10% |\n| Ground-floor slab casting | 20% |\n| 10th-floor slab casting | 20% |\n| Completion of superstructure | 20% |\n| Offer of possession | 20% + additional charges |\n\nFor example, on a hypothetical base price of ₹3.27 crore:\n\n<box border radius=\"xl\" padding={3} gap={3}>\n  <row align=\"center\">\n    <box flex=\"1\" gap={1}>\n      <text color=\"secondary\" size=\"sm\">Booking payment (10%)</text>\n      <title size=\"xl\">₹32.7 lakh</title>\n    </box>\n    <box flex=\"1\" gap={1}>\n      <text color=\"secondary\" size=\"sm\">Within 30 days (10%)</text>\n      <title size=\"xl\">₹32.7 lakh</title>\n    </box>\n  </row>\n  <divider color=\"subtle\"/>\n  <text color=\"secondary\" size=\"sm\">The next three construction instalments would each be ₹65.4 lakh. The final ₹65.4 lakh would be due at possession, along with applicable additional charges.</text>\n</box>\n\nThe developer's published price information lists the new project's 3BHK starting price at approximately ₹3.27 crore before additional charges. That is already above your previously stated ₹2 crore budget. <Cite refs={[\"turn371217search3\",\"turn371217search8\"]}/>\n\nOfficial project website: [ACE Arte](https://acearte.in/)\n\n## 2. Original ACE Parkway: historical developer plans\n\nACE Group's published project page lists these schedules for specific towers. These are not a guarantee of the terms available for a flat today. <Cite refs={[\"turn530543search5\"]}/>\n\n| Tower | Published payment schedule |\n|---|---|\n| Tower 10 | 50% within 45 days; 50% on offer of possession |\n| Towers 6, 7 and 8 | 20% within 45 days; 15% at the 19th-floor slab; 65% on offer of possession |\n\nSince the original project is delivered, if you buy a resale flat, the seller's terms, outstanding dues, loan arrangements and transfer requirements will determine when you pay.\n\n## What I would do in your position\n\nBefore paying an expression-of-interest amount or booking fee, get the dated price sheet and full cost sheet directly from ACE Group. Confirm how the initial payment is adjusted, whether GST and other charges are extra, and whether the schedule matches the agreement and the project's UP-RERA record.\n\nOne important question before we go further:\n\n**Are you looking at the original, ready-to-move ACE Parkway, or the new ACE Arte project (ACE Parkway 2.0)?**\n\nThat determines whether we should calculate your upfront cash requirement for a resale purchase or evaluate the new project's construction-linked payment plan."
                ]
            },
            "status": "finished_successfully",
            "end_turn": true,
            "weight": 1.0,

                    "constants": {
                        "0": "There are ",
                        "1": "two different projects you need to distinguish before looking at the payment plan",
                        "2": ":",

                        "4": "ACE Parkway",
                        "5": "3ffebd24-1b61-5e95-8b90-4667c80853e6",
                        "6": " — original project",
                        "7": "Ready to move",
                        "8": "The original project is delivered. If you buy a flat from the developer's remaining inventory, if any, or from an existing owner, the applicable payment terms will differ. The developer's published page lists tower-specific historical plans. ",

                        "13": "8b156f63-e995-50c6-9723-932c37fb8cb4",
                        "14": " — marketed as ACE Parkway 2.0",

                        "16": "This is a separate, under-construction project with its own RERA registration and payment schedule. If someone has offered you a new flat with staged payments, this may be the project they mean. ",

                        "18": "942d2c4a-6ecd-545a-a687-b8420b25b9e3",
                        "19": "1. ACE Parkway 2.0 / ACE Arte: current published payment plan",
                        "20": "The published plan is called ",
                        "21": "20×5",
                        "22": ", meaning five instalments of 20% of the base price. The first instalment is split into two payments. ",

                        "33": "10th-floor slab casting",

                        "36": "20%",
                        "37": "Offer of possession",

                        "39": "For example, on a hypothetical base price of ₹3.27 crore:",
                        "40": "Booking payment (10%)",

                        "43": "₹32.7 lakh",
                        "44": "The next three construction instalments would each be ₹65.4 lakh. The final ₹65.4 lakh would be due at possession, along with applicable additional charges.",
                        "45": "The developer's published price information lists the new project's 3BHK starting price at approximately ₹3.27 crore before additional charges. That is already above your previously stated ₹2 crore budget. ",

                        "49": "25bfac6e-fbcd-5e68-a028-1e13eb99de53",
                        "50": "ACE Arte",
                        "51": "2. Original ACE Parkway: historical developer plans",
                        "52": "ACE Group's published project page lists these schedules for specific towers. These are not a guarantee of the terms available for a flat today. ",

Confirm how the initial payment is adjusted, whether GST and other charges are extra, and whether the schedule matches the agreement and the project's UP-RERA record.",
                        "64": "One important question before we go further:",
                        "65": "Are you looking at the original, ready-to-move ACE Parkway, or the new ACE Arte project (ACE Parkway 2.0)?",
                        "66": "That determines whether we should calculate your upfront cash requirement for a resale purchase or evaluate the new project's construction-linked payment plan."
                    },
                    "fallbackMarkdown": "There are **two different projects you need to distinguish before looking at the payment plan**\\:\n\nimage_group{\"query\":[\"ACE Parkway completed residential apartment towers Sector 150 Noida\"],\"aspect_ratio\":\"4:3\",\"layout\":\"carousel\",\"num_per_query\":1}\n\n**entity[\"turn371217business0\",\"ACE Parkway\"] — original project**\n\n(Ready to move)\n\nThe original project is delivered. If you buy a flat from the developer's remaining inventory, if any, or from an existing owner, the applicable payment terms will differ. The developer's published page lists tower-specific historical plans. citeturn530543search5turn371217search1\n\n---\n\nimage_group{\"query\":[\"ACE Arte ACE Parkway 2.0 Sector 150 Noida new luxury residential project architectural rendering\"],\"aspect_ratio\":\"4:3\",\"layout\":\"carousel\",\"num_per_query\":1}\n\n**entity[\"turn371217business1\",\"ACE Arte\"] — marketed as ACE Parkway 2.0**\n\n(New launch)\n\nThis is a separate, under-construction project with its own RERA registration and payment schedule. If someone has offered you a new flat with staged payments, this may be the project they mean. citeturn371217search1turn371217search8\n\n## 1\\. ACE Parkway 2.0 / ACE Arte: current published payment plan\n\nThe published plan is called **20×5**, meaning five instalments of 20% of the base price. The first instalment is split into two payments. citeturn371217search0turn371217search8\n\n| Payment stage | Amount due |\n|--------|--------|\n| On booking | 10% |\n| Within 30 days of booking | 10% |\n| Ground-floor slab casting | 20% |\n| 10th-floor slab casting | 20% |\n| Completion of superstructure | 20% |\n| Offer of possession | 20% + additional charges |\n\nFor example, on a hypothetical base price of ₹3.27 crore\\:\n\nBooking payment (10%)\n\n# ₹32.7 lakh\n\nWithin 30 days (10%)\n\n# ₹32.7 lakh\n\n---\n\nThe next three construction instalments would each be ₹65.4 lakh. The final ₹65.4 lakh would be due at possession, along with applicable additional charges.\n\nThe developer's published price information lists the new project's 3BHK starting price at approximately ₹3.27 crore before additional charges. That is already above your previously stated ₹2 crore budget. citeturn371217search3turn371217search8\n\nOfficial project website: urlACE Artehttps://acearte.in/\n\n## 2\\. Original ACE Parkway: historical developer plans\n\nACE Group's published project page lists these schedules for specific towers. These are not a guarantee of the terms available for a flat today. citeturn530543search5\n\n| Tower | Published payment schedule |\n|--------|--------|\n| Tower 10 | 50% within 45 days; 50% on offer of possession |\n| Towers 6, 7 and 8 | 20% within 45 days; 15% at the 19th-floor slab; 65% on offer of possession |\n\nSince the original project is delivered, if you buy a resale flat, the seller's terms, outstanding dues, loan arrangements and transfer requirements will determine when you pay.\n\n## What I would do in your position\n\nBefore paying an expression-of-interest amount or booking fee, get the dated price sheet and full cost sheet directly from ACE Group. Confirm how the initial payment is adjusted, whether GST and other charges are extra, and whether the schedule matches the agreement and the project's UP-RERA record.\n\nOne important question before we go further\\:\n\n**Are you looking at the original, ready-to-move ACE Parkway, or the new ACE Arte project (ACE Parkway 2.0)?**\n\nThat determines whether we should calculate your upfront cash requirement for a resale purchase or evaluate the new project's construction-linked payment plan.",
